   62: def main():
   63:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Execute Adaptive-V11 candidates on fresh black-box MAS systems")
   64:     ap.add_argument("--round", type=int, required=True)
   65:     ap.add_argument("--split", choices=["train", "dev"], default="train")
   66:     ap.add_argument("--root", default="artifacts/adaptive_v11_active_v1")
   67:     ap.add_argument("--manifest", default="artifacts/adaptive_v11_active_v1/manifest_privileged.json")
   68:     ap.add_argument("--model", default=None)
   69:     ap.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=4, help="Concurrent API requests; keep modest for rate limits")
   70:     ap.add_argument("--retries", type=int, default=3)
   71:     args = ap.parse_args()
   72: 
   73:     if not os.environ.get("OPENAI_API_KEY"):
   74:         raise SystemExit("OPENAI_API_KEY is not set")
   75:     root = Path(args.root)
   76:     cand_path = root / f"candidates_{args.split}_round_{args.round:02d}.jsonl"
   77:     candidates = read_jsonl(cand_path)
   78:     manifest = json.loads(Path(args.manifest).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   79:     by_id = {r["session_id"]: r for r in manifest["systems"] if r["split"] == args.split}
   80:     if not candidates:
   81:         raise SystemExit(f"no candidates in {cand_path}")
   82: 
   83:     public_cache_path = root / "target_public_cache.jsonl"
   84:     truth_cache_path = root / "target_truth_cache_training_only.jsonl"
   85:     public_cache = load_cache(public_cache_path)
   86:     truth_cache = load_cache(truth_cache_path)
   87:     rows_by_pos: dict[int, dict] = {}
   88:     pending = {}
   89: 
   90:     def make_row(c, m, output, selected, *, hit, status, retries_used=0):
   91:         sid = str(c["session_id"])
   92:         return {
   93:             **c,
   94:             "system_id": sid,
   95:             "final_output": str(output),
   96:             "output": str(output),
   97:             "answer": str(output),
   98:             "selected_roles_training_only": list(map(str, selected)),
   99:             "truth_roles_training_only": list(map(str, m["roles"])),
  100:             "manifest_sha256": manifest["sha256"],
  101:             "mode": m["mode"],
  102:             "execution_status": status,
  103:             "cache_hit": bool(hit),
  104:             "retries_used": int(retries_used),
  105:             "action": {"target": f"adaptive_r{args.round:02d}", "competitor": None, "variant": int(c["candidate_index"])},
  106:         }
  107: 
  108:     with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=max(1, args.workers)) as ex:
  109:         for pos, c in enumerate(candidates):
  110:             sid = str(c["session_id"])
  111:             if sid not in by_id:
  112:                 raise RuntimeError(f"candidate references unknown session {sid}")
  113:             m = dict(by_id[sid]); m["split"] = args.split
  114:             q = str(c.get("query", ""))
  115:             if not c.get("valid"):
  116:                 rows_by_pos[pos] = make_row(c, m, "", [], hit=False, status="invalid_generation")
  117:                 continue
  118:             key = cache_key(sid, q)
  119:             if key in public_cache and key in truth_cache:
  120:                 rows_by_pos[pos] = make_row(
  121:                     c, m, public_cache[key]["output"], truth_cache[key].get("selected_roles", []),
  122:                     hit=True, status="ok",
  123:                 )
  124:                 continue
  125:             fut = ex.submit(_execute_one, m, sid, q, args.model, args.retries)
  126:             pending[fut] = (pos, c, m, key)
  127: 
  128:         done = len(rows_by_pos)
  129:         for fut in as_completed(pending):
  130:             pos, c, m, key = pending[fut]
  131:             sid = str(c["session_id"]); q = str(c["query"])
  132:             output, selected, retries_used = fut.result()
  133:             pub = {"key": key, "session_id": sid, "query": q, "output": output}
  134:             prv = {
  135:                 "key": key, "session_id": sid,
  136:                 "query_hash": hashlib.sha256(q.encode()).hexdigest(),
  137:                 "selected_roles": selected, "true_roles": list(map(str, m["roles"])),
  138:             }
  139:             append_jsonl(public_cache_path, pub); append_jsonl(truth_cache_path, prv)
  140:             public_cache[key] = pub; truth_cache[key] = prv
  141:             rows_by_pos[pos] = make_row(c, m, output, selected, hit=False, status="ok", retries_used=retries_used)
  142:             done += 1
  143:             print(f"[execute] {done}/{len(candidates)} split={args.split} r={args.round} sid={sid} cand={c['candidate_index']} retries={retries_used}", flush=True)
  144: 
  145:     rows = [rows_by_pos[i] for i in range(len(candidates))]
  146:     out = root / f"responses_{args.split}_round_{args.round:02d}.jsonl"
  147:     write_jsonl(out, rows)
  148:     hits = sum(bool(r.get("cache_hit")) for r in rows)
  149:     print(f"[done] responses={out} rows={len(rows)} cache_hits={hits} workers={args.workers}", flush=True)
  150: 
  151: if __name__ == "__main__": main()

  110: 
  111:             rb = adaptive_role_reward(

  113:                 new_role_probs=new_roles,
  114:                 true_roles=true_roles,
  115:                 query=str(c.get("query", "")),
  116:                 previous_queries=[h.query for h in state.history],
  117:                 generator_valid=bool(c.get("valid")),
  118:             )
  119:             evaluated.append((c, frame, new_k, new_roles, rb))
  120: 
  121:         rewards = [x[4].total for x in evaluated]
  122:         relative_advs = group_relative_advantages(rewards)
  123: 
  124:         # V14A_G50_SIGN_CONSISTENT_ADVANTAGE
  125:         # Preserve GRPO ranking, but force the gradient direction to agree
  126:         # with ABSOLUTE realized inference utility.
  127:         #
  128:         #   reward > 0 : never punish a genuinely helpful probe
  129:         #   reward < 0 : never reinforce a harmful "best-of-bad" probe
  130:         #   reward = 0 : no policy-gradient signal
  131:         advs = [
  132:             max(0.0, float(a)) if float(r) > 0.0
  133:             else min(0.0, float(a)) if float(r) < 0.0
  134:             else 0.0
  135:             for a, r in zip(relative_advs, rewards)
  136:         ]
  137:         for (c, frame, new_k, new_roles, rb), adv in zip(evaluated, advs):
  138:             if args.split == "train" and c.get("completion"):

  147:                     "ref_logp": float(c["ref_logp"]),

  150:                     "reward_breakdown_training_only": rb.to_dict(),

  153:         viable = [x for x in evaluated if x[1] is not None]
  154:         if not viable:

  156:             # Preserve the previous public belief/history instead of
  157:             # crashing the whole experiment or fabricating evidence.
  158:             print(
  159:                 f"[advance-warning] no viable candidate in {sid}; "

  162:             )

  215:         write_jsonl(root / f"grpo_train_round_{args.round:02d}.jsonl", train_rows)

   14:     ap.add_argument("--round", type=int, required=True)
   15:     ap.add_argument("--split", choices=["train", "dev"], default="train")

   22:     out = root / f"scored_{args.split}_round_{args.round:02d}.jsonl"
   23:     if not inp.exists():
   24:         raise SystemExit(f"missing response file: {inp}")

   26:     if not valid_rows:
   27:         raise RuntimeError("no valid executed responses to score")

   31:     cmd = [sys.executable, args.scorer, input_flag, str(score_input), output_flag, str(out), *extra]
   32:     print("[score-bridge]", " ".join(cmd), flush=True)
   33:     subprocess.run(cmd, check=True)
   34:     if not out.exists() or out.stat().st_size == 0:
   35:         raise RuntimeError(f"dual scorer returned successfully but did not create {out}")
   36:     print(f"[done] scored={out}", flush=True)

FILE: scripts/v14a_role_advance_g50_v2.py  bucket_hits=1 total_score=12

ARGPARSE/ENTRYPOINT CONTEXT:
   43:         "oracle_k_role_recall": float(oracle_k_recall),
   44:     }
   45: 
   46: 
   47: def main():
   48:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Role-only belief update + truth-only GRPO reward")
   49:     ap.add_argument("--round", type=int, required=True)
   50:     ap.add_argument("--split", choices=["train", "dev"], default="train")
   51:     ap.add_argument("--root", default="artifacts/adaptive_v11_role_only_v1")
   52:     ap.add_argument("--belief", default="artifacts/adaptive_v11_active_v1/frozen_belief.joblib")
   53:     ap.add_argument("--manifest", default="artifacts/adaptive_v11_active_v1/manifest_privileged.json")
   54:     ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=984311)
   55:     args = ap.parse_args()
   56: 
   57:     root = Path(args.root)
   58:     states = {
   59:         r["session_id"]: PublicBeliefState.from_public_dict(r)
   60:         for r in read_jsonl(root / f"states_{args.split}_round_{args.round-1:02d}.jsonl")
   61:     }
   62:     candidates = read_jsonl(root / f"candidates_{args.split}_round_{args.round:02d}.jsonl")
   63:     responses = read_jsonl(root / f"responses_{args.split}_round_{args.round:02d}.jsonl")
   64:     scored = read_jsonl(root / f"scored_{args.split}_round_{args.round:02d}.jsonl")
   65:     manifest = json.loads(Path(args.manifest).read_text())
   66:     truth = {r["session_id"]: r for r in manifest["systems"] if r["split"] == args.split}
   67:     belief = FrozenBeliefBundle.load(args.belief)
   68: 
   69:     cand_by = {(r["session_id"], int(r["candidate_index"])): r for r in candidates}
   70:     resp_by = {(r["session_id"], int(r["candidate_index"])): r for r in responses}
   71:     score_by = {}
   72:     for r in scored:
   73:         sid = str(r.get("system_id", r.get("session_id", "")))
   74:         var = int(dict(r.get("action", {})).get("variant", r.get("candidate_index", 0)))
   75:         score_by[(sid, var)] = r
   76: 
   77:     grouped = defaultdict(list)
   78:     for (sid, _), c in cand_by.items():
   79:         grouped[sid].append(c)
   80: 
   81:     train_rows = []
   82:     next_states = []
   83:     metrics = []
   84: 
   85:     for sid, cs in sorted(grouped.items()):
   86:         state = states[sid]
   87:         t = truth[sid]
   88:         true_roles = tuple(map(str, t["roles"]))
   89:         evaluated = []
   90: 
   91:         for c in sorted(cs, key=lambda x: int(x["candidate_index"])):
   92:             idx = int(c["candidate_index"])
   93:             r = resp_by[(sid, idx)]
   94:             sc = score_by.get((sid, idx))
   95:             if c.get("valid") and r.get("execution_status") == "ok" and sc is None:
   96:                 raise RuntimeError(f"missing scorer output sid={sid} cand={idx}")
   97: 
   98:             if c.get("valid") and r.get("execution_status") == "ok" and sc is not None:
   99:                 frame = EvidenceFrame(
  100:                     query=str(c["query"]),
  101:                     answer=str(r.get("final_output", "")),
  102:                     v135={str(k): float(v) for k, v in dict(sc.get("evidence", {})).items()},
  103:                     v134={str(k): float(v) for k, v in dict(sc.get("metadata", {}).get("evidence_v134", {})).items()},
  104:                 )
  105:                 new_history = [*state.history, frame]
  106:                 new_k, new_roles = role_only_predict(belief, new_history)
  107:             else:
  108:                 frame = None
  109:                 new_k, new_roles = dict(state.k_probs), dict(state.role_probs)
  110: 
  111:             rb = adaptive_role_reward(
  112:                 old_role_probs=state.role_probs,
  113:                 new_role_probs=new_roles,
  114:                 true_roles=true_roles,
  115:                 query=str(c.get("query", "")),
  116:                 previous_queries=[h.query for h in state.history],
  117:                 generator_valid=bool(c.get("valid")),
  118:             )
  119:             evaluated.append((c, frame, new_k, new_roles, rb))
  120: 
  121:         rewards = [x[4].total for x in evaluated]
  122:         relative_advs = group_relative_advantages(rewards)
  123: 
  124:         # V14A_G50_SIGN_CONSISTENT_ADVANTAGE
  125:         # Preserve GRPO ranking, but force the gradient direction to agree
  126:         # with ABSOLUTE realized inference utility.
  127:         #
  128:         #   reward > 0 : never punish a genuinely helpful probe
  129:         #   reward < 0 : never reinforce a harmful "best-of-bad" probe
  130:         #   reward = 0 : no policy-gradient signal
  131:         advs = [
  132:             max(0.0, float(a)) if float(r) > 0.0
  133:             else min(0.0, float(a)) if float(r) < 0.0
  134:             else 0.0
  135:             for a, r in zip(relative_advs, rewards)
  136:         ]
  137:         for (c, frame, new_k, new_roles, rb), adv in zip(evaluated, advs):
  138:             if args.split == "train" and c.get("completion"):
  139:                 train_rows.append({
  140:                     "session_id": sid,
  141:                     "round": args.round,
  142:                     "candidate_index": int(c["candidate_index"]),
  143:                     "group_id": c["group_id"],
  144:                     "prompt": c["prompt"],
  145:                     "completion": c["completion"],
  146:                     "old_logp": float(c["old_logp"]),
  147:                     "ref_logp": float(c["ref_logp"]),
  148:                     "reward": float(rb.total),
  149:                     "advantage": float(adv),
  150:                     "reward_breakdown_training_only": rb.to_dict(),
  151:                 })
  152: 
  153:         viable = [x for x in evaluated if x[1] is not None]
  154:         if not viable:
  155:             # No usable observation for this session in this round.
  156:             # Preserve the previous public belief/history instead of
  157:             # crashing the whole experiment or fabricating evidence.
  158:             print(
  159:                 f"[advance-warning] no viable candidate in {sid}; "
  160:                 f"carrying previous belief forward",
  161:                 flush=True,
  162:             )
  163: 
  164:             ns = PublicBeliefState(
  165:                 session_id=sid,
  166:                 split=args.split,
  167:                 round_index=args.round,
  168:                 k_probs=dict(state.k_probs),
  169:                 role_probs=dict(state.role_probs),
  170:                 history=list(state.history),
  171:             )
  172:             ns.validate(TRAIN_ROLES, belief.ks)
  173:             next_states.append(ns.to_public_dict())
  174: 
  175:             rm = role_metrics(state.role_probs, true_roles)
  176:             metrics.append({
  177:                 "session_id": sid,
  178:                 "split": args.split,
  179:                 "round": args.round,
  180:                 "chosen_candidate": -1,
  181:                 "chosen_reward_training_only": 0.0,
  182:                 "probe_status": "no_viable_candidate",
  183:                 **rm,
  184:             })
  185:             continue
  186: 
  187:         # Continue from a designated on-policy sample independent of reward.
  188:         rng = random.Random(stable_int(args.seed, args.split, args.round, sid))
  189:         chosen = viable[rng.randrange(len(viable))]
  190:         c, frame, new_k, new_roles, rb = chosen
  191:         ns = PublicBeliefState(
  192:             session_id=sid,
  193:             split=args.split,
  194:             round_index=args.round,
  195:             k_probs=dict(new_k),  # deliberately uniform in phase 1
  196:             role_probs=dict(new_roles),
  197:             history=[*state.history, frame],
  198:         )
  199:         ns.validate(TRAIN_ROLES, belief.ks)
  200:         next_states.append(ns.to_public_dict())
  201: 
  202:         rm = role_metrics(new_roles, true_roles)
  203:         metrics.append({
  204:             "session_id": sid,
  205:             "split": args.split,
  206:             "round": args.round,
  207:             "chosen_candidate": int(c["candidate_index"]),
  208:             "chosen_reward_training_only": float(rb.total),
  209:             **rm,
  210:         })
  211: 
  212:     write_jsonl(root / f"states_{args.split}_round_{args.round:02d}.jsonl", next_states)
  213:     write_jsonl(root / f"metrics_{args.split}_round_{args.round:02d}.jsonl", metrics)
  214:     if args.split == "train":
  215:         write_jsonl(root / f"grpo_train_round_{args.round:02d}.jsonl", train_rows)
  216: 
  217:     summary = {
  218:         "phase": "role_only",
  219:         "split": args.split,
  220:         "round": args.round,
  221:         "sessions": len(metrics),
  222:         "mean_role_log_loss": float(np.mean([x["role_log_loss"] for x in metrics])),
  223:         "mean_role_brier": float(np.mean([x["role_brier"] for x in metrics])),
  224:         "mean_role_auc": float(np.nanmean([x["role_auc"] for x in metrics])),
  225:         "mean_true_false_margin": float(np.mean([x["true_false_margin"] for x in metrics])),
  226:         "mean_oracle_k_role_recall": float(np.mean([x["oracle_k_role_recall"] for x in metrics])),
  227:         "note": "K is intentionally uniform and is not used in policy state updates or reward during phase 1.",
  228:     }
  229:     write_json(root / f"advance_{args.split}_round_{args.round:02d}_summary.json", summary)
  230:     print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2), flush=True)
  231: 
  232: 
  233: if __name__ == "__main__":
  234:     main()

  107:             if c.get('valid') and rr and rr.get('execution_status') == 'ok' and sc is not None:
  108:                 frame = EvidenceFrame(
  109:                     query=str(c['query']),
  110:                     answer=str(rr.get('final_output', '')),
  111:                     v135={str(k): float(v) for k, v in dict(sc.get('evidence', {})).items()},
  112:                     v134={str(k): float(v) for k, v in dict(sc.get('metadata', {}).get('evidence_v134', {})).items()},
  113:                 )
  114:                 nh = [*st.history, frame]
  115:                 nr = role_only_predict(bundle, nh)
  116:             else:
  117:                 nh = list(st.history)
  118:                 nr = dict(st.role_probs)
  119: 
  120:             rb = curriculum_reward(
  121:                 old_probs=st.role_probs,
  122:                 new_probs=nr,
  123:                 true_roles=true_roles,
  124:                 roles=TRAIN_ROLES,
  125:                 query=str(c.get('query', '')),
  126:                 previous_queries=[h.query for h in st.history],
  127:                 generator_valid=bool(c.get('valid') and frame is not None),
  128:             )
  129:             evals.append((c, frame, nr, rb))
  130: 
  131:         rewards = [float(e[3].total) for e in evals]
  132:         advs = shaped_group_advantages(rewards)
  133:         chosen_item = None
  134:         chosen_reward = None
  135:         chosen_idx = None
  136: 
  137:         for (c, frame, nr, rb), adv in zip(evals, advs):
  138:             chosen = bool(c.get('chosen_for_continuation')) and frame is not None
  139:             if chosen:
  140:                 chosen_item = (c, frame, nr, rb)
  141:                 chosen_reward = float(rb.total)
  142:                 chosen_idx = int(c['candidate_index'])
  143: 
  144:             if a.split == 'train' and c.get('completion'):
  145:                 train_rows.append(
  146:                     {
  147:                         'session_id': sid,
  148:                         'round': a.round,
  149:                         'candidate_index': int(c['candidate_index']),
  150:                         'group_id': c['group_id'],
  151:                         'prompt': c['prompt'],
  152:                         'completion': c['completion'],
  153:                         'old_logp': float(c['old_logp']),
  154:                         'ref_logp': float(c['ref_logp']),
  155:                         'reward': float(rb.total),
  156:                         'local_advantage': float(adv),
  157:                         'chosen_for_continuation': bool(chosen),
  158:                         'selector_predicted_utility': float(c.get('selector_predicted_utility', float('nan'))),
  159:                         'selector_model_samples': int(c.get('selector_model_samples', 0)),
  160:                         'selector_mode': str(c.get('selector_mode', 'unknown')),
  161:                         'reward_breakdown_training_only': rb.to_dict(),
  162:                     }
  163:                 )
  164: 
  165:             if a.split == 'train' and frame is not None and bool(c.get('valid')):
  166:                 selector_X.append(feature_vector(st, str(c.get('query', '')), int(c['candidate_index'])))
  167:                 selector_y.append(float(rb.total))
  168: 
  169:         if chosen_item is None:
  170:             ns = PublicBeliefState(
  171:                 session_id=sid,
  172:                 split=a.split,
  173:                 round_index=a.round,
  174:                 k_probs=dummy_k_probs(bundle),
  175:                 role_probs=dict(st.role_probs),
  176:                 history=list(st.history),
  177:             )
  178:             status = 'carry_no_valid_chosen'
  179:         else:
  180:             c, frame, nr, rb = chosen_item
  181:             ns = PublicBeliefState(
  182:                 session_id=sid,
  183:                 split=a.split,
  184:                 round_index=a.round,
  185:                 k_probs=dummy_k_probs(bundle),
  186:                 role_probs=dict(nr),
  187:                 history=[*st.history, frame],
  188:             )
  189:             status = 'ok'
  190: 
  191:         ns.validate(TRAIN_ROLES, bundle.ks)
  192:         next_states.append(ns.to_public_dict())
  193:         metrics.append(
  194:             {
  195:                 'session_id': sid,
  196:                 'round': a.round,
  197:                 'status': status,
  198:                 'role_log_loss': -role_log_score(ns.role_probs, true_roles, TRAIN_ROLES),
  199:                 'role_brier': role_brier(ns.role_probs, true_roles, TRAIN_ROLES),
  200:                 'role_auc': auc_simple(ns.role_probs, true_roles),
  201:                 'true_false_margin': margin(ns.role_probs, true_roles, TRAIN_ROLES),
  202:                 'oracle_k_role_recall': topk_recall(ns.role_probs, true_roles),
  203:             }
  204:         )
  205: 
  206:         finite_rewards = [r for (c, frame, nr, rb), r in zip(evals, rewards) if frame is not None]
  207:         if finite_rewards:
  208:             best_reward = float(max(finite_rewards))
  209:             worst_reward = float(min(finite_rewards))
  210:             if chosen_reward is not None:
  211:                 rank = 1 + sum(1 for r in finite_rewards if r > chosen_reward)
  212:                 regret = best_reward - chosen_reward
  213:             else:
  214:                 rank = None
  215:                 regret = None
  216:             selection_diags.append(
  217:                 {
  218:                     'session_id': sid,
  219:                     'round': a.round,
  220:                     'chosen_candidate': chosen_idx,
  221:                     'chosen_reward': chosen_reward,
  222:                     'best_reward': best_reward,
  223:                     'worst_reward': worst_reward,
  224:                     'regret': regret,
  225:                     'chosen_rank': rank,
  226:                     'pool_size': len(finite_rewards),
  227:                     'pool_has_positive': bool(best_reward > 0.0),
  228:                 }
  229:             )
  230: 
  231:     write_jsonl(root / f"states_{a.split}_round_{a.round:02d}.jsonl", next_states)

  238:         # Idempotent selector update: update id is persisted atomically inside the .npz model.
  239:         update_key = f"{root.resolve()}|stage={a.stage}|round={a.round}"
  240:         update_hash = hashlib.sha256(update_key.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()
  241:         selector_summary = update_model(
  242:             a.selector_model, selector_X, selector_y, update_id=update_hash
  243:         )

  245: 
  246:     chosen_vals = [x['chosen_reward'] for x in selection_diags if x.get('chosen_reward') is not None]
  247:     regrets = [x['regret'] for x in selection_diags if x.get('regret') is not None]
  248:     summary = {

  251:         'round': a.round,

  255:         'valid_state_updates': sum(x['status'] == 'ok' for x in metrics),
  256:         'pool_positive_fraction': float(np.mean([x['pool_has_positive'] for x in selection_diags])) if selection_diags else float('nan'),
  257:         'mean_best_reward': float(np.mean([x['best_reward'] for x in selection_diags])) if selection_diags else float('nan'),
  258:         'mean_chosen_reward': float(np.mean(chosen_vals)) if chosen_vals else float('nan'),
  259:         'mean_selector_regret': float(np.mean(regrets)) if regrets else float('nan'),

  610:

  619:     seeds = [7, 17, 29]

  621:

  623:         print("\n" + "=" * 96)
  624:         print(f"SEED {seed}")
  625:         print("=" * 96)

  627:         meta = {}
  628:

  655:         "dev_sessions": len(set(g.sid for g in dev_groups)),
  656:         "semantic_embedding_dim": emb_dim,
  657:         "aggregate": aggregate,
  658:         "seeds": all_results,
  659:         "important_limitation": (
  660:             "Offline evaluation is one-step counterfactual at logged histories. "
  661:             "Multi-step adaptive AUC trajectories require new closed-loop MAS executions."
  662:         ),

  669:     print("=" * 96)
  670:     for mode in modes:
  671:         a = aggregate[mode]
  672:         print(f"\n{mode.upper()}")
  673:         for key in [
  674:             "mean_delta_auc_learned",
  675:             "mean_auc_after_learned",

   72:     for r in scored:

   74:         var = int(dict(r.get("action", {})).get("variant", r.get("candidate_index", 0)))
   75:         score_by[(sid, var)] = r

   79:         grouped[sid].append(c)

   96:                 raise RuntimeError(f"missing scorer output sid={sid} cand={idx}")

  102:                     v135={str(k): float(v) for k, v in dict(sc.get("evidence", {})).items()},

  117:                 generator_valid=bool(c.get("valid")),
  118:             )
  119:             evaluated.append((c, frame, new_k, new_roles, rb))
  120: 
  121:         rewards = [x[4].total for x in evaluated]
  122:         relative_advs = group_relative_advantages(rewards)
  123: 
  124:         # V14A_G50_SIGN_CONSISTENT_ADVANTAGE
  125:         # Preserve GRPO ranking, but force the gradient direction to agree
  126:         # with ABSOLUTE realized inference utility.
  127:         #
  128:         #   reward > 0 : never punish a genuinely helpful probe
  129:         #   reward < 0 : never reinforce a harmful "best-of-bad" probe
  130:         #   reward = 0 : no policy-gradient signal
  131:         advs = [
  132:             max(0.0, float(a)) if float(r) > 0.0
  133:             else min(0.0, float(a)) if float(r) < 0.0
  134:             else 0.0
  135:             for a, r in zip(relative_advs, rewards)
  136:         ]
  137:         for (c, frame, new_k, new_roles, rb), adv in zip(evaluated, advs):
  138:             if args.split == "train" and c.get("completion"):

  141:                     "round": args.round,

  152: 
  153:         viable = [x for x in evaluated if x[1] is not None]
  154:         if not viable:

   24: def role_metrics(role_probs: dict[str, float], true_roles: tuple[str, ...]) -> dict:
   25:     truth = set(true_roles)

   80: 
   81:     train_rows = []
   82:     next_states = []

   84: 
   85:     for sid, cs in sorted(grouped.items()):

   87:         t = truth[sid]

   89:         evaluated = []
   90: 
   91:         for c in sorted(cs, key=lambda x: int(x["candidate_index"])):

   93:             r = resp_by[(sid, idx)]
   94:             sc = score_by.get((sid, idx))
   95:             if c.get("valid") and r.get("execution_status") == "ok" and sc is None:

   97:

  103:                     v134={str(k): float(v) for k, v in dict(sc.get("metadata", {}).get("evidence_v134", {})).items()},
  104:                 )

  110:

  116:                 previous_queries=[h.query for h in state.history],
  117:                 generator_valid=bool(c.get("valid")),
  118:             )
  119:             evaluated.append((c, frame, new_k, new_roles, rb))
  120: 
  121:         rewards = [x[4].total for x in evaluated]

  123:         for (c, frame, new_k, new_roles, rb), adv in zip(evaluated, advs):
  124:             if args.split == "train" and c.get("completion"):

  136:                     "reward_breakdown_training_only": rb.to_dict(),
  137:                 })
  138: 
  139:         viable = [x for x in evaluated if x[1] is not None]
  140:         if not viable:
  141:             # No usable observation for this session in this round.
  142:             # Preserve the previous public belief/history instead of
  143:             # crashing the whole experiment or fabricating evidence.
  144:             print(
  145:                 f"[advance-warning] no viable candidate in {sid}; "
  146:                 f"carrying previous belief forward",
  147:                 flush=True,
  148:             )
  149:

  169:                 **rm,
  170:             })
  171:             continue
  172: 
  173:         # Continue from a designated on-policy sample independent of reward.
  174:         rng = random.Random(stable_int(args.seed, args.split, args.round, sid))
  175:         chosen = viable[rng.randrange(len(viable))]
  176:         c, frame, new_k, new_roles, rb = chosen
  177:         ns = PublicBeliefState(
  178:             session_id=sid,
  179:             split=args.split,
  180:             round_index=args.round,

  184:         )

  189:         metrics.append({
  190:             "session_id": sid,
  191:             "split": args.split,
  192:             "round": args.round,
  193:             "chosen_candidate": int(c["candidate_index"]),
  194:             "chosen_reward_training_only": float(rb.total),
  195:             **rm,
  196:         })
  197: 
  198:     write_jsonl(root / f"states_{args.split}_round_{args.round:02d}.jsonl", next_states)
  199:     write_jsonl(root / f"metrics_{args.split}_round_{args.round:02d}.jsonl", metrics)
  200:     if args.split == "train":
  201:         write_jsonl(root / f"grpo_train_round_{args.round:02d}.jsonl", train_rows)
  202:

  205:         "split": args.split,
  206:         "round": args.round,
  207:         "sessions": len(metrics),
  208:         "mean_role_log_loss": float(np.mean([x["role_log_loss"] for x in metrics])),
  209:         "mean_role_brier": float(np.mean([x["role_brier"] for x in metrics])),
  210:         "mean_role_auc": float(np.nanmean([x["role_auc"] for x in metrics])),

  214:     }

   24: def role_metrics(role_probs: dict[str, float], true_roles: tuple[str, ...]) -> dict:
   25:     truth = set(true_roles)
   26:     ys = np.asarray([int(r in truth) for r in TRAIN_ROLES], dtype=np.int64)
   27:     ps = np.asarray([min(1-EPS, max(EPS, float(role_probs[r]))) for r in TRAIN_ROLES], dtype=np.float64)
   28:     ll = float(np.mean(-(ys*np.log(ps)+(1-ys)*np.log1p(-ps))))
   29:     brier = float(np.mean((ps-ys)**2))
   30:     auc = float(roc_auc_score(ys, ps)) if len(set(ys.tolist())) == 2 else float("nan")
   31:     pos = float(ps[ys == 1].mean()) if np.any(ys == 1) else float("nan")
   32:     neg = float(ps[ys == 0].mean()) if np.any(ys == 0) else float("nan")
   33:     k = len(truth)
   34:     topk = {r for r, _ in sorted(role_probs.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1], reverse=True)[:k]}

   24: def role_metrics(role_probs: dict[str, float], true_roles: tuple[str, ...]) -> dict:
   25:     truth = set(true_roles)
   26:     ys = np.asarray([int(r in truth) for r in TRAIN_ROLES], dtype=np.int64)
   27:     ps = np.asarray([min(1-EPS, max(EPS, float(role_probs[r]))) for r in TRAIN_ROLES], dtype=np.float64)
   28:     ll = float(np.mean(-(ys*np.log(ps)+(1-ys)*np.log1p(-ps))))
   29:     brier = float(np.mean((ps-ys)**2))
   30:     auc = float(roc_auc_score(ys, ps)) if len(set(ys.tolist())) == 2 else float("nan")
   31:     pos = float(ps[ys == 1].mean()) if np.any(ys == 1) else float("nan")
   32:     neg = float(ps[ys == 0].mean()) if np.any(ys == 0) else float("nan")
   33:     k = len(truth)
   34:     topk = {r for r, _ in sorted(role_probs.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1], reverse=True)[:k]}
   35:     oracle_k_recall = len(topk & truth) / max(1, k)
   36:     return {

   24: def role_metrics(role_probs: dict[str, float], true_roles: tuple[str, ...]) -> dict:
   25:     truth = set(true_roles)
   26:     ys = np.asarray([int(r in truth) for r in TRAIN_ROLES], dtype=np.int64)
   27:     ps = np.asarray([min(1-EPS, max(EPS, float(role_probs[r]))) for r in TRAIN_ROLES], dtype=np.float64)
   28:     ll = float(np.mean(-(ys*np.log(ps)+(1-ys)*np.log1p(-ps))))
   29:     brier = float(np.mean((ps-ys)**2))
   30:     auc = float(roc_auc_score(ys, ps)) if len(set(ys.tolist())) == 2 else float("nan")
   31:     pos = float(ps[ys == 1].mean()) if np.any(ys == 1) else float("nan")
   32:     neg = float(ps[ys == 0].mean()) if np.any(ys == 0) else float("nan")
   33:     k = len(truth)
   34:     topk = {r for r, _ in sorted(role_probs.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1], reverse=True)[:k]}
   35:     oracle_k_recall = len(topk & truth) / max(1, k)
   36:     return {

   53:     ap.add_argument('--root', required=True)
   54:     ap.add_argument('--manifest', required=True)
   55:     ap.add_argument('--belief', required=True)
   56:     ap.add_argument('--selector-model', required=True)
   57:     ap.add_argument('--stage', type=int, default=1)
   58:     a = ap.parse_args()
   59: 
   60:     root = Path(a.root)
   61:     states = {
   62:         x['session_id']: PublicBeliefState.from_public_dict(x)
   63:         for x in read_jsonl(root / f"states_{a.split}_round_{a.round-1:02d}.jsonl")
   64:     }
   65:     cand = read_jsonl(root / f"candidates_{a.split}_round_{a.round:02d}.jsonl")
   66:     resp = read_jsonl(root / f"responses_{a.split}_round_{a.round:02d}.jsonl")
   67:     scored = read_jsonl(root / f"scored_{a.split}_round_{a.round:02d}.jsonl")
   68:     mani = json.loads(Path(a.manifest).read_text())
   69:     truth = {x['session_id']: x for x in mani['systems'] if x['split'] == a.split}
   70:     bundle = FrozenBeliefBundle.load(a.belief)
   71: 
   72:     resp_by = {
   73:         (
   74:             str(x.get('session_id', x.get('system_id', ''))),
   75:             int(x.get('candidate_index', dict(x.get('action', {})).get('variant', 0))),
   76:         ): x
   77:         for x in resp
   78:     }
   79:     score_by = {}
   80:     for x in scored:
   81:         sid = str(x.get('system_id', x.get('session_id', '')))
   82:         idx = int(dict(x.get('action', {})).get('variant', x.get('candidate_index', 0)))
   83:         score_by[(sid, idx)] = x
   84: 
   85:     grouped = defaultdict(list)
   86:     for x in cand:
   87:         grouped[x['session_id']].append(x)
   88: 
   89:     train_rows = []
   90:     next_states = []
   91:     metrics = []
   92:     selector_X = []
   93:     selector_y = []
   94:     selection_diags = []
   95: 
   96:     for sid, cs in sorted(grouped.items()):
   97:         st = states[sid]
   98:         tr = truth[sid]
   99:         true_roles = tuple(map(str, tr['roles']))
  100:         evals = []
  101: 
  102:         for c in sorted(cs, key=lambda x: int(x['candidate_index'])):
  103:             idx = int(c['candidate_index'])
  104:             rr = resp_by.get((sid, idx))
  105:             sc = score_by.get((sid, idx))
  106:             frame = None
  107:             if c.get('valid') and rr and rr.get('execution_status') == 'ok' and sc is not None:
  108:                 frame = EvidenceFrame(
  109:                     query=str(c['query']),
  110:                     answer=str(rr.get('final_output', '')),
  111:                     v135={str(k): float(v) for k, v in dict(sc.get('evidence', {})).items()},
  112:                     v134={str(k): float(v) for k, v in dict(sc.get('metadata', {}).get('evidence_v134', {})).items()},
  113:                 )
  114:                 nh = [*st.history, frame]
  115:                 nr = role_only_predict(bundle, nh)
  116:             else:
  117:                 nh = list(st.history)
  118:                 nr = dict(st.role_probs)
  119: 
  120:             rb = curriculum_reward(
  121:                 old_probs=st.role_probs,
  122:                 new_probs=nr,
  123:                 true_roles=true_roles,
  124:                 roles=TRAIN_ROLES,
  125:                 query=str(c.get('query', '')),
  126:                 previous_queries=[h.query for h in st.history],
  127:                 generator_valid=bool(c.get('valid') and frame is not None),
  128:             )
  129:             evals.append((c, frame, nr, rb))
  130: 
  131:         rewards = [float(e[3].total) for e in evals]
  132:         advs = shaped_group_advantages(rewards)
  133:         chosen_item = None
  134:         chosen_reward = None
  135:         chosen_idx = None
  136: 
  137:         for (c, frame, nr, rb), adv in zip(evals, advs):
  138:             chosen = bool(c.get('chosen_for_continuation')) and frame is not None
  139:             if chosen:
  140:                 chosen_item = (c, frame, nr, rb)
  141:                 chosen_reward = float(rb.total)
  142:                 chosen_idx = int(c['candidate_index'])
  143: 
  144:             if a.split == 'train' and c.get('completion'):
  145:                 train_rows.append(
  146:                     {
  147:                         'session_id': sid,
  148:                         'round': a.round,
  149:                         'candidate_index': int(c['candidate_index']),
  150:                         'group_id': c['group_id'],
  151:                         'prompt': c['prompt'],
  152:                         'completion': c['completion'],
  153:                         'old_logp': float(c['old_logp']),
  154:                         'ref_logp': float(c['ref_logp']),
  155:                         'reward': float(rb.total),
  156:                         'local_advantage': float(adv),
  157:                         'chosen_for_continuation': bool(chosen),
  158:                         'selector_predicted_utility': float(c.get('selector_predicted_utility', float('nan'))),
  159:                         'selector_model_samples': int(c.get('selector_model_samples', 0)),
  160:                         'selector_mode': str(c.get('selector_mode', 'unknown')),
  161:                         'reward_breakdown_training_only': rb.to_dict(),
  162:                     }
  163:                 )
  164: 
  165:             if a.split == 'train' and frame is not None and bool(c.get('valid')):
  166:                 selector_X.append(feature_vector(st, str(c.get('query', '')), int(c['candidate_index'])))
  167:                 selector_y.append(float(rb.total))
  168: 
  169:         if chosen_item is None:
  170:             ns = PublicBeliefState(
  171:                 session_id=sid,
  172:                 split=a.split,
  173:                 round_index=a.round,
  174:                 k_probs=dummy_k_probs(bundle),
  175:                 role_probs=dict(st.role_probs),
  176:                 history=list(st.history),
  177:             )
  178:             status = 'carry_no_valid_chosen'
  179:         else:
  180:             c, frame, nr, rb = chosen_item
  181:             ns = PublicBeliefState(
  182:                 session_id=sid,
  183:                 split=a.split,
  184:                 round_index=a.round,
  185:                 k_probs=dummy_k_probs(bundle),
  186:                 role_probs=dict(nr),
  187:                 history=[*st.history, frame],
  188:             )
  189:             status = 'ok'
  190: 
  191:         ns.validate(TRAIN_ROLES, bundle.ks)
  192:         next_states.append(ns.to_public_dict())
  193:         metrics.append(
  194:             {
  195:                 'session_id': sid,
  196:                 'round': a.round,
  197:                 'status': status,
  198:                 'role_log_loss': -role_log_score(ns.role_probs, true_roles, TRAIN_ROLES),
  199:                 'role_brier': role_brier(ns.role_probs, true_roles, TRAIN_ROLES),
  200:                 'role_auc': auc_simple(ns.role_probs, true_roles),
  201:                 'true_false_margin': margin(ns.role_probs, true_roles, TRAIN_ROLES),
  202:                 'oracle_k_role_recall': topk_recall(ns.role_probs, true_roles),
  203:             }
  204:         )
  205: 
  206:         finite_rewards = [r for (c, frame, nr, rb), r in zip(evals, rewards) if frame is not None]
  207:         if finite_rewards:
  208:             best_reward = float(max(finite_rewards))
  209:             worst_reward = float(min(finite_rewards))
  210:             if chosen_reward is not None:
  211:                 rank = 1 + sum(1 for r in finite_rewards if r > chosen_reward)
  212:                 regret = best_reward - chosen_reward
  213:             else:
  214:                 rank = None
  215:                 regret = None
  216:             selection_diags.append(
  217:                 {
  218:                     'session_id': sid,
  219:                     'round': a.round,
  220:                     'chosen_candidate': chosen_idx,
  221:                     'chosen_reward': chosen_reward,
  222:                     'best_reward': best_reward,
  223:                     'worst_reward': worst_reward,
  224:                     'regret': regret,
  225:                     'chosen_rank': rank,
  226:                     'pool_size': len(finite_rewards),
  227:                     'pool_has_positive': bool(best_reward > 0.0),
  228:                 }
  229:             )
  230: 
  231:     write_jsonl(root / f"states_{a.split}_round_{a.round:02d}.jsonl", next_states)
  232:     write_jsonl(root / f"metrics_{a.split}_round_{a.round:02d}.jsonl", metrics)
  233:     write_jsonl(root / f"selector_diag_{a.split}_round_{a.round:02d}.jsonl", selection_diags)
  234: 
  235:     selector_summary = None
  236:     if a.split == 'train':
  237:         write_jsonl(root / f"train_rows_round_{a.round:02d}.jsonl", train_rows)
  238:         # Idempotent selector update: update id is persisted atomically inside the .npz model.
  239:         update_key = f"{root.resolve()}|stage={a.stage}|round={a.round}"
  240:         update_hash = hashlib.sha256(update_key.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()
  241:         selector_summary = update_model(
  242:             a.selector_model, selector_X, selector_y, update_id=update_hash
  243:         )
  244:         selector_summary['update_key'] = update_key
  245: 
  246:     chosen_vals = [x['chosen_reward'] for x in selection_diags if x.get('chosen_reward') is not None]
  247:     regrets = [x['regret'] for x in selection_diags if x.get('regret') is not None]
  248:     summary = {
  249:         'split': a.split,
  250:         'stage': int(a.stage),
  251:         'round': a.round,
  252:         'systems': len(metrics),
  253:         'train_rows': len(train_rows),
  254:         'mean_role_log_loss': float(np.mean([x['role_log_loss'] for x in metrics])) if metrics else float('nan'),
  255:         'valid_state_updates': sum(x['status'] == 'ok' for x in metrics),
  256:         'pool_positive_fraction': float(np.mean([x['pool_has_positive'] for x in selection_diags])) if selection_diags else float('nan'),
  257:         'mean_best_reward': float(np.mean([x['best_reward'] for x in selection_diags])) if selection_diags else float('nan'),

  259:         'mean_selector_regret': float(np.mean(regrets)) if regrets else float('nan'),

  261:         'selector_model': selector_summary,
  262:     }
  263:     write_json(root / f"advance_{a.split}_round_{a.round:02d}_summary.json", summary)

   20: def auc_simple(probs, true_roles):
   21:     truth = set(true_roles)
   22:     pos = [probs[r] for r in TRAIN_ROLES if r in truth]
   23:     neg = [probs[r] for r in TRAIN_ROLES if r not in truth]
   24:     if not pos or not neg:
   25:         return float('nan')
   26:     wins = sum((a > b) + 0.5 * (a == b) for a in pos for b in neg)
   27:     return wins / (len(pos) * len(neg))

   32:     top = sorted(TRAIN_ROLES, key=lambda r: probs[r], reverse=True)[:k]

  646:         all_results.append({"seed": seed, "models": model_results, "training": meta})
  647: 
  648:     aggregate = aggregate_seed_results(all_results)

   50:

   52:     for key, c in cand_by.items(): grouped[key[0]].append(c)

   58:         state = states[sid]

   63:         for c in sorted(cs, key=lambda x: int(x["candidate_index"])):
   64:             idx = int(c["candidate_index"])
   65:             r = resp_by[(sid, idx)]
   66:             sc = score_by.get((sid, idx))
   67:             if c.get("valid") and r.get("execution_status") == "ok" and sc is None:
   68:                 raise RuntimeError(f"missing scored observation sid={sid} cand={idx}")

   80:                 new_history = list(state.history)
   81:                 new_k, new_roles = dict(state.k_probs), dict(state.role_probs)

   83:             rb = adaptive_reward(

   91:                 previous_queries=[h.query for h in state.history],
   92:                 generator_valid=bool(c.get("valid")),
   93:             )
   94:             evaluated.append((c, frame, new_k, new_roles, rb))
   95: 
   96:         rewards = [x[4].total for x in evaluated]

   98:         for (c, frame, new_k, new_roles, rb), adv in zip(evaluated, advs):
   99:             if args.split == "train" and c.get("completion"):

  101:                     "session_id": sid,
  102:                     "round": args.round,

  111:                     "reward_breakdown_training_only": rb.to_dict(),
  112:                 })
  113: 
  114:         # Critical: continuation is independent of reward. Uniform over successfully observed
  115:         # policy samples; best-of-G continuation would create optimistic off-policy states.
  116:         viable = [x for x in evaluated if x[1] is not None]
  117:         if not viable:
  118:             raise RuntimeError(f"no viable candidate for trajectory continuation in {sid}")
  119:         rng = random.Random(stable_int(args.seed, args.split, args.round, sid))
  120:         chosen = viable[rng.randrange(len(viable))]
  121:         c, frame, new_k, new_roles, rb = chosen
  122:         ns = PublicBeliefState(
  123:             session_id=sid,
  124:             split=args.split,
  125:             round_index=args.round,
  126:             k_probs=dict(new_k),
  127:             role_probs=dict(new_roles),

  129:         )

  132:         pred_set = predicted_set(new_roles, new_k)

  135:             "split": args.split,
  136:             "round": args.round,

  139:             "true_k_eval_only": true_k,
  140:             "pred_k": int(map_k(new_k)),
  141:             "k_correct": int(map_k(new_k) == true_k),
  142:             "role_f1": float(f1(pred_set, set(true_roles))),
  143:             "exact_set": int(pred_set == set(true_roles)),
  144:         })
  145: 
  146:     write_jsonl(root / f"states_{args.split}_round_{args.round:02d}.jsonl", next_states)

  148:     if args.split == "train":
  149:         write_jsonl(root / f"grpo_train_round_{args.round:02d}.jsonl", train_rows)

  152:         "round": args.round,
  153:         "sessions": len(metrics),
  154:         "k_acc": sum(x["k_correct"] for x in metrics) / max(1, len(metrics)),
  155:         "mean_role_f1": sum(x["role_f1"] for x in metrics) / max(1, len(metrics)),
  156:         "exact_set": sum(x["exact_set"] for x in metrics) / max(1, len(metrics)),
  157:         "note": "truth appears only in reward/metrics; next policy-visible state excludes it",
